<div class="wotd-label">📖 Word of the day</div>
<div class="wotd-word">gnomic <span class="wotd-pos">adjective</span></div>
<div class="wotd-def">Gnomic is a formal word that describes something cryptically said or written in few words.</div>
<div class="wotd-ex">The painter is notorious for offering terse, gnomic responses to questions about her work that leave interviewers and critics scratching their heads.</div>
